OK so the actual problem: c_k = ∑_n h[n+k](1-h[n]) — define b_k = ∑_n h[n+k](1-h[n]) for k = -(n-1)...n-1. Note b_k = (∑_n 1·1) ... b_k = # of pairs of distance -k (left index n+k, right n) where left=1, right=0. So C5 = max_k b_k dx. Total over k: ∑_k b_k = (# of ones)(# of zeros) = (n/2)². For n=250: total 15625, mean over (2n-1) = 499 shifts: average b_k = 31.3, ×dx = 0.2505. Hence lower bound C5 ≥ 0.2505 (discrete). At optimum probably C5 ~ 0.38 ⇒ b_max ~ 47.5.

Now the problem becomes: under ∑h = n/2 and 0 ≤ h ≤ 1, balance the pairwise cross-correlation across distances in a bounded interval: b_k = ∑_n h[n+k](1-h[n]) for all k (with truncation at the ends), minimize max.

This is like a "difference set" problem! b_k counts weighted 1→0 pair within offset k. Define A = 1 (support) and B = 0. b_k = #{(i, j): j - i = -k, i ∈ A, j ∈ B}, where i = n (index of 1-h), j = n+k: j - i = k: pair (i ∈ B? ...) define: h[n+k]=1 (j = n+k "one side"? confusing) — forget it: b_k = number of pairs (u, v) with u - v = k, h[u] = 1 (well, h[u] = 1), h[v] = 0 (well). Think of 0 as point "colored G", 1 as "colored H"; b_k = # directed G→H pairs with displacement k (right index minus left index is… u minus v is k). Symmetric.

Goal: color (fractionally color) each of n sites with color G (h=0) / H (h=1) such that #G→H is ≤ M for all k. Total # of G→H pairs over all displacement = (#G)(#H) (all pairs counted exactly once by displacement). To have all per-displacement ≤ M, we need to spread pair-displacements uniformly: displacement multiset should be balanced like a "Sidon-ish complete difference set". In the binary case, for H-set S (size m = n/2), B = complement: b_k = # { (g, h): g = h - k, g ∉ S, h ∈ S } = number of S-elements h such that h-k ∉ S. So b_k = m - a_k, where a_k = # {h ∈ S: h - k ∈ S} = (S * reversed S autocorrelation) for positive k, similarly negative with direction. And C5 = max_k (m - a_k^-) dx, where a^- is directional. For binary, at k=0 C5: 0. So C5 = max_{k≠0} (m - min directional autocorrelation). For uniform b ~ m²/(2n), m - a ~ n/8: a ~ m - n/8 = n/4 … for n=250: b ~ 31.3 ⇒ a_k ≈ 93.7. So need autocorrelation a_k ≥ m - 47.5 = 77.5 (threshold (n/2) - 0.3808/0.008 = 125 - 47.6 = 77.4). Hmm, but for fractional h, h is H intensity: generalize.

In the sum: ∑ 1 = n-k; ∑(-u_i) over i range = -∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} u_i; ∑ u_j over j = k..n-1 = ∑_{j=k}^{n-1} u_j; -∑ u_i u_j with separation k. So b_k = (n-k)/4 - (1/4)[ -∑_{n-k..} + ∑_{0..k-1} ] → (n-k)/4 - (1/4)[ ∑_{j=0}^{k-1} u_j + ∑_{i=n-k}^{n-1} u_i? sign: with -u_i term: -(1/4)(∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} u_i) = (1/4) ∑_{i=n-k}^{n-1} u_i. And ~(1/4)∑_{j=k}^{n-1} u_j = -(1/4) ∑_{j=0}^{k-1} u_j. So b_k = (1/4)[(n-k) + (tail) - (head) - C_k], where C_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} u_i u_{i+k}.

Minimize max of b_k over all k: want about C_k + head − tail ≈ ... for small k, head, tail ~ ∑ of k u's ~ O(k·mean) — if u is oscillatory O(1)? b_k ≈ (n-k)/4 - (1/4)C_k + small. Improved ideas:
1. Adjustable max / adaptive smoothing parameter with better numerical Hessian (e.g. use L-BFGS-B with analytical gradient of the smooth max, via projection onto {0≤h≤1, ∑h=n/2} as a few Bregman-like iterations (dynamic Bregman / dual ascent)). Better: use a second-order / active-set method via smooth-max (softmax) with rigorous gradient and L-BFGS-B, then projected active-set minimization restricted to active lags (truncated SLSQP). Min-max at optimum: active lags tied at value M; reduce to a set of linear-ish constraints b_k ≤ M for active set k, minimize M: that's a linear program in h and M!! Wait b_k is bilinear in h? b_k = ∑ h[n+k](1-h[n]) = -∑h[n+k]h[n] + ∑h[n+k]: the h h cross-term is quadratic (but not same-index: cross-term of distinct coordinates). So LP not linear. Bilinear. Hmm.

But note: if h is binary (0/1), b_k is linear! Because h·1 = h. So for a binary h, b_k is bilinear… for a binary, product h_i h_j with i ≠ j remains bilinear. OK.

Alternative approach: view as min over h of max_k (∑_{i} h_i h_{i+k} - ∑ h shifted ...). Bilinear min-max. Standard techniques: alternate: for fixed h, active set = argmax b_k; optimize h by gradient descent with constraints; or use cutting plane: at iterate h_t, add linearization of b_k at h_t for all k: b_k ≈ b_k(h_t) + ∇b_k(h_t)·(h - h_t) ≤ M_t; solve the LP (bilinear approximation) with constraints and 0 ≤ h ≤ 1, sum = n/2, minimize M_t; that's a linear program (in h and M): L cuts. This "cutting-plane / Kelley" approach for bilinear min-max works well and can be solved with CVXPY + CBC/GLPK for n=250 with possibly hundreds of cuts, per LP: 250 variables + 1, cuts ≤ few hundred — fast (GLPK okay, CBC okay). Then alternate h with cutting plane (outer: update cuts) (inner: solve LP, project continuity). This is essentially "solving the outer envelope with b_k linearizations" — a relaxation of the true bilinear, but the cuts at a point are exact upper bounds: b_k(h) ≤ b_k(h_t) + ∇b_k(h_t)(h - h_t), since b_k is multi-affine (convex? no: bilinear is not convex in pairs of variables; multi-affine is convex on the hypercube along a line but the linearization gives an upper bound in each coordinate direction only when the other argument is fixed in the bilinear monomial and the product term is ≥? For multi-affine f(x): in x_i, with other arguments fixed: f is linear → linearization = exact w.r.t. x_i. The joint linearization (all variables) equals f at x_t and for a multi-affine b_k, which is convex along any line... f multi-affine is not jointly convex; the sum of single-coordinate tangent planes is a valid upper bound for x ∈ [0,1]^n?? For multi-affine f: f(x) ≤ ∑_i [f(x with only x_i) - ...]? There's a known fact: any multi-affine polynomial is bounded above by its "linear upper hull" from any point via coordinate-wise... hmm: for a multi-linear f on a box, the multilinear extension is convex along any axis-direction, but not joint. The linearization at x_t: L(x) = ∑_i ∂f/∂x_i(x_t)(x_i - x_t) + f(x_t). Is f(x) ≤ L(x) on the box? For a multi-affine f in 2 variables: f = x y, x_t = (0.5, 0.5): L(x,y) = 0.5y + 0.5x - 0.25. f ≤ L? At (1,1): f = 1, L = 0.75 < 1 → not upper bound. So plain cuts fail.

Fix: use individualized-coordinate upper bounds (like Dinkelbach / McCormick): b_k(h) = ∑ bilinear-term; each bilinear term h_i h_j is bounded by McCormick on [0,1]²: h_i h_j ≤ ... McCormick upper: min(h_i, h_j) ≤ h_i, ≤ h_j: upper bound ∈ {h_i, h_j, h_i + h_j - 1} (tightest = h_i + h_j - 1 when that's smaller… McCormick upper envelope is min of three planes: u ≤ x, u ≤ y, u ≤ x + y - 1). "Min of affine" — can't use directly in LP, but choose one plane at cut time: at point x_t, McCormick tangent: the max of the linear tight pieces at that point is a valid upper bound on the box. At each cut iteration, b_k's upper piece approximation: for each bilinear term, select the tight plane at h_t. Then b_k ≤ selected planes sum (valid upper on the entire box). Use that for active lags as LP cuts: solve min M subject to b_k^{ub}(h) ≤ M (linear), 0≤h≤1, ∑h=n/2. Iterate. This is a valid outer approximation! And the classification of the bilinear term xy by the plane improves as h_t progresses; at a 0/1 optimum, the upper plane equals the exact (if h_t binary, the tight planes give exact value at that vertex). This method is like Benders / outer approximation; typically converges to a good solution. To avoidLP complexity, use projected subgradient / saddle: minimize over h max_k b_k directly with batch gradient over top lags.

Practically: let's solve by a strong numerical optimizer with a smart target: Treat as min-max of bilinear quantities b_k (k = 0..n-1, both directions via sign of k internally). Use:
- Fitting to target: minimize ∥b_k - M∥ (for k in 0..n-1 and both signs?) plus C5 — actually, we can formulate directly: minimize over h of max_k b_k + λ·variance of b_k, smooth max approximation with large alpha (like α = 5000–50000) so the full Pareto vector is guided. Better than SLSQP: use projected gradient/L-BFGS:
  * Standard smoothmax: F(h) = M + (1/α) log ∑ exp(α(b_k - M)): gradient wrt h: (1 in derivate) ∑_k e^{α(b_k - M)}/∑e^… ∂b_k/∂h = softmax-weighted of b: ∑_k p_k ∇b_k: O(n²) per gradient recompute via convolution: ∇b_k wrt h: b_k = ∑_i h_{i+k}(1 - h_i): ∂/∂h_j = sum over i = j, i+k = j for terms… 2 terms: -h_j? b_k = -∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i h_{i+k} + ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_{i+k}. ∂b_k/∂h_j: (-∑ over i: if i = j: h_{i+k} = h_{j+k}; if i + k = j: h_i = h_{j-k}): ⇒ -h_{j+k} (when valid) - h_{j-k} (when valid) + 1 (when j ∈ [k, n-1]). So ∇_j F = ∑_k p_k [valid? 1 - (j≥k? h_{j-k} : 0) - (j+k ≤ n-1? h_{j+k} : 0)] — this is a linear combination: ∑ p_k 1_{j≥k, j ≤ n-1} - (h * p) direct - (h * p reversed), correlated by p vector → O(n log n) per gradient via FFT/convolve.
  * Then L-BFGS-B with constraints handled by parameterization + log-barrier or projection: projection onto ∑h = n/2, 0 ≤ h ≤ 1: soft (barrier) with interior point, or projection as a line-search step with bisection t (projection after each step ≈ constraint violation tiny...) For L-BFGS, keep feasibility in the interior: parameterize h = sigmoid((n/2 - …)·z scaled)? sum constraint: hard. Alternative: h = sigmoid(a·(z + θ z-sum-c))? Ensure ∑ = n/2: add shift φ solving ∑ sigmoid(a z + φ) = n/2: 1D bisection per (a, z): cheap (n ops). So h = σ(a z + φ(z)), |u| < 1 the domain (interior); as a → ∞ the binary limit. Objective F(h(z)) compute by gradient chain: ∂h/∂z = σ'(a z + φ)·a + ∂φ term: ∂φ/∂z_i such that ∂h_i/∂φ·∂φ/∂z_i + ... = 0: σ'(x_i)·∂φ/∂z_i + a σ'(x_i) = 0 ⇒ ∂φ/∂z_i = -a. So ∂h_i/∂z_j = δ_ij a σ_i' - a σ_i' = a σ_i' (δ_ij - 1).

This is a clean smooth interior method: F(z), h(z) feasible (bounds and mean up to solver remainder at a → ∞; at finite a, h is strictly interior so bounds OK). Objective F = smooth max of b with alpha: gradient straightforward via correlation.

Grid scales: n = 250; target ~0.38: each evaluation O(n log n) is trivial. Can afford tons of iterations within 1100 sec. L-BFGS on the smoothmax with alpha ~ 1500-3000 in log-space: many local optima possible; use random restarts (z random, a ramp from modest 2-3 to 30+). Also "annealed max": increase alpha gradually (continuation trick): anneal alpha from 50 → 2500 in stages, re-optimizing each — continuation dramatically helps.

Even better: include both directions: b_k for k = -(n-1) .. n-1: full "bilinear correlation": define B(h) = corr(h, 1-h)^full (2n-1 values). F = smoothmax over all 2n-1 lags. At k = 0 the term ∝ (h_i)² — z's parameterization is smooth.

Gradient detail (F = smooth-softmax over full 2n-1 lags of b_k, α):
b over all shifts k ∈ [-(n-1), n-1]: vector of length 2n-1: b[k] = ∑_i h_i h_{i+k} (-sign) + ... let's define L = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full') * dx (natively, via numpy convolution? — gradient needs analytic: F = α·logsumexp(b - m, b) = m + lx, m = max b. dF/dh_i = (1/α? no) softmax weight: p_k = exp(α(b_k - m))/Z: dF/dh_i = ∑_k p_k ∂b_k/∂h_i.

Broad convention for b (for numpy correlate(h, g, 'full')[i + (n-1)]): value v_k (i = k + (n-1)): v_k = ∑_n h[n+k] (1 - h[n]) for k ∈ [-(n-1), n-1], index valid only (n, n+k ∈ [0, n)). Equivalently v_k = [where valid, (1-h) * h-shifted]: v_k = ∑_i (1-h)[i] · h[i+k] = full conv (1-h) * h-reversed at... directly: V = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full') computed. And analytically: v_k = S1_k - S2_k, where S1_k = ∑_{valid i} h[i+k] = truncated window sum of = h, R_k = ∑_{valid i} h[i] h[i+k] convolution: v_k: coefficient for k=0: (1/4)? (irrelevant if we compute v directly: v_k = (n - |k|) terms? Numerically trivial for gradient:
v_k = 
 ∑_i (1-h_i) h_{i+k}:
∂v_k/∂h_j = -[j+...wait: sum over i: term i: (1 - h_i) h_{i+k}:
  * i = j: term (1 - h_j) h_{j+k}: derivative: -(1)·h_{j+k} → -h_{j+k} (if j + k valid)!
  * i + k = j, i.e., i = j - k: term (1 - h_{j-k}) h_j: derivative: (1 - h_{j-k}).
  So ∂v_k/∂h_j = (1 - h_{j-k})·1_{valid j-k} - h_{j+k}·1_{valid j+k}.
cross-correlation: for fixed k, the vector is (flip of (1-h)) * h. Sum over k with weight p_k:
G_j = ∑_k p_k [(1 - h_{j-k}) 1_{j-k ∈ range, and i = j-k valid} - h_{j+k} 1_{j+k valid}]
validity: (i = j - k valid: 0 ≤ i < n) ∧ (j valid trivial) ⇒ for given k: condition i valid: 0 ≤ j - k < n. First piece: for k ∈ [-(n-1) -? j - k ≥ 0 ⇒ k ≤ j; j - k ≤ n-1 ⇒ k ≥ j - n + 1 ⇒ k ∈ [max? With k ∈ [-(n-1), n-1] fixed j: range: (1 - h_{j-k}), 0 - hmm, i ∈ [0, n-1] required: k ∈ [j - (n-1), j]: define k_pos = k - j? Then A_j = ∑_{i} (1-h_i) p_{j-i} = ∑_i v[i] u[j - i], v = 1-h: = fullconv(u, v)[j + ...] hmm, fullconv(u, v)[m] = ∑_i u[i] v[m - i] (u length 2n-1): need ∑_i v[i] u[j - i] (substitute i' = j - i): = ∑_{i'} u[i'] v[j - i'] ⇒ m = j. So A = np.convolve(p_shifted, 1-h, mode='full') index j. ✓.
B_j = ∑_k p_k h_{j + k} (valid j + k): = ∑_k p'_{k'} h_{j - k'}, k' = -k: B_j = ∑_{k'} p_{-k'} h_{j - k'}… B = ∑_{l} p_{-l} h_{j - l}: = convolve(p_neg_shifted, h, 'full')[j], p_neg[q] = p_{- (q - (n-1))}? Index: p_neg_shifted (length 2n-1): index l in (shifted): coefficient p_{- l_s}, l_s = l - (n - 1) → coefficient p_{(n-1) - l}. So p_rev = reverse? B = convolve(np.flip(p), h, 'full')[j]: flip: np.flip(p)[u] = p[(2n-2) - u] → coefficient at l_s = (n-1) - u? And np.flip(p) index u corresponds to p index (2n-2) - u: p_{(2n-2) - u}; want p_{(n-1) - l_s} (l_s = l - (n-1)); set u = (n-1) + l_s → (2n-2) - (n-1) - l_s = (n-1) - l_s ✓. And fullconv(np.flip(p), h)[m] = ∑_u flip(p)[u] h[m - u] = ∑ h[m - u] p_{n-1-u}: m - u = j - l_s ⇒ u = (n-1) + l_s: = ∑_l? h[j - l_s] p_{(n-1) - l_s} ✓ = B_j.
So G_j = A_j - B_j, A = fullconv(p_shifted, 1-h)[j], B = fullconv(flip(p), h)[j]: O(n) with np.convolve (both are length 2n-1 and n: full length 3n-2: ~2·n² ops = 125k mults: trivial).

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def C5_local(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    v = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(v.max() * dx)

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(h.copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(400):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h; s = room.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + diff * room / s, 0, 1)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + diff * h / s, 0, 1)
    return h

def smooth_grad(h, n, alpha):
    v = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = v.max()
    z = v - m
    e = np.exp(alpha * z)
    t = e.sum()
    c5 = dx-like: v.max() * dx + ... Care in using max(logsumexp/alpha) (dx · (m + lse/alpha)):

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _c5_of(h, n):
    v = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(v)) * (2.0 / n), v

def _c5val(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full'))) * (2.0 / n)

def _project(h, n):
    h = np.clip(h, 0, 1)
    t = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(500):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-12: break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h; s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            h = np.clip(h + d * r / s, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            h = np.clip(h + d * h / s, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def _sig(x):
    out = np.empty_like(x)
    pos = x >= 0
    out[pos] = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-x[pos]))
    e = np.exp(x[~pos]); out[~pos] = e / (1.0 + e)
    return out

def _phi_newton(z, a):
    phi = 0.0
    for _ in range(12):
        s = _sig(a * z + phi)
        g = s.sum() - 0.5 * len(z)
        gp = (s * (1.0 - s)).sum()
        if gp <= 0: break
        step = g / gp
        phi -= step
        if abs(step) < 1e-13: break
    return phi

def _h_of_z(z, a):
    return _sig(a * z + _phi_newton(z, a))

def _Fgrad(z, a, alpha):
    n = len(z)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = _h_of_z(z, a)
    v = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = float(v.max())
    w = np.exp(alpha * (v - m))
    Z = w.sum()
    p = w / Z
    F = (m + np.log(Z) / alpha) * dx
    A = np.convolve(1.0 - h, p, mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    B = np.convolve(h, np.flip(p), mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    G = A - B
    sg = a * h * (1.0 - h)
    dF = dx * sg * (G - float(sg @ G))
    return F, dF
```

```python
def _anneal(z0, a0, n, t_end, seeds_key):
    stages = [(2,20),(3,60),(5,150),(8,350),(12,800),(18,1800),(28,4000),(45,9000)]
    z = z0
    for (a, al) in stages:
        if time.time() > t_end: break
        res = minimize(_optfun, z, args=(a, al), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                       options={'maxiter':400,'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12})
        z = res.x
    h = _project(_h_of_z(z, a), n)
    return h, _c5val(h, n)
```

def _multi_anneal(h0, n, rng, t_end):
    best_h, best_c = h0.copy(), _c5val(h0, n)
    starts = [_logit(h0), (h0 - 0.5) * 6.0, rng.normal(0,1,n), sine...family]:
    for s in starts:
        if time.time() > t_end - 20: break
        per = min(35, (t_end - time.time()))
        h, c = _anneal(s, 2, n, time.time() + per)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12: best_c, best_h = c, h
    return best_h, best_c

```python
def _make_starts(h0, n, rng):
    starts = []
    eps = 1e-6
    hc = np.clip(h0, eps, 1.0 - eps)
    starts.append(np.log(hc / (1.0 - hc)))
    starts.append((h0 - 0.5) * 6.0)
    starts.append((h0 - 0.5) * 2.0 + rng.normal(0, 0.6, n))
    for f in (2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
        phi = np.pi * f * 0.5 + rng.uniform(0, 1.0)
        starts.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + phi))
    starts.append(rng.normal(0, 1.0, n))
    return starts
```

_Pair:
```python
def _pair(h, n, t_end, deltas, radius=None):
    dx = 2.0/n
    v0 = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')
    best = float(v0.max())
    r = radius
    for d in deltas:
        while time.time() < t_end:
            imp = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() >= t_end: break
                jlo = 0 if r is None else max(1, i - r)
                jhi = n if r is None else min(n - 1, i + r)
                for j in range(i+1, jhi):
                    if h[i] + d <= 1 and h[j] - d >= 0:
                        h[i] += d; h[j] -= d
                        c = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')))*dx
                        if c < best - 1e-15: best = c; imp = True
                        else:
                            h[i] -= d; h[j] += d
            if not imp: break
    return h, best*dx
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full'))) * (2.0 / n)

def _vvec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def _proj(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    t = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(300):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s <= 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + d * r / s, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s <= 1e-14:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + d * h / s, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def _sig(x):
    y = np.empty_like(x)
    p = x >= 0
    y[p] = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-x[p]))
    e = np.exp(x[~p])
    y[~p] = e / (1.0 + e)
    return y

def _zphi(z, a):
    s = 0.5 * len(z)
    ph = np.sum(np.logaddexp(0.0, a*z)) hmm...

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def c5_of(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project(h, n):
    h = np.clip(h.copy(), 0.0, 1.0)
    t = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(400):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + d * r / s, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + d * h / s, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def _sig(a, z, phi):
    x = a * z + phi
    out = np.empty_like(x)
    m = x < 0
    e = np.exp(x[m]); out[m] = e/(1 + e)
    out[~m] = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-x[~m]))
    return out

def phi_of(z, a):
    phi = float(np.mean(z)) * 0  # 0
    sz = 0.5 * len(z)
    for _ in range(30):
        s = _sig(a, z, phi)
        g = s.sum() - sz
        if abs(g) < 1e-14:
            break
        gp = float((s * (1.0 - s)).sum())
        if gp < 1e-15:
            break
        phi -= g / gp
    return phi

def h_of(z, a):
    return _sig(a, z, phi_of(z, a))

def fgrad_z(z, a, alpha):
    n = len(z)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = h_of(z, a)
    vh = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = float(vh.max())
    zexp = alpha * (vh - m)
    w = np.exp(zexp)
    tw = w.sum()
    p = w / tw
    F = dx * (m + np.log(tw) / alpha)
    P = np.zeros(2 * n - 1); P[n - 1:n - 1 + (2 * n - 1)] = p   # hmm: p length 2n-1, shift (n-1) family correct: P = p (index: lag k ↔ q = k + n - 1): p家庭的 (2n−1)...numpy correlate (h, 1-h, 'full')[q] = lag k = q - (n-1): P vector q = 0..2n-2 = P[k + n-1]·…: P = p ✓ (explicitly): P = p copy: don't shift (lag convention already in index with h/…: A = convolve((1-h), P, 'full')[j + n − 1]·…: derive: Σ_(i) (1 - h_i) p_{j - i} (j - k…+…p lag number (index A valid): p_lag, lag index: P_lagidx, full conv (len P = 2n−1, len h = n): cv[j + n − 1] = Σ_i h_i·P[j+ n - 1 - i]·…: uhh: coefficient index: P family idx = lag + (n − 1)·...: cv[j + n − 1] = Σ_i (1-h_i) P[j + n − 1 − i]·…: P index j + n − 1 − i = (j − i) → lag (j - i), need (j − k…target A_j = Σ_i (1 − h_i) p_{j - i} ✓ regardless of lag-permute index??: for P family, at index q lag (q − (n−1)) family...p lag number (index A valid)…(j − k…+ P q = j + n − 1 − i → lag (j − n + 1 − i + n − 1) = j − i ✓: A_j = cv[n−1 + j]. ✓ OK.
    A = np.convolve(1.0 - h, p, mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    B = np.convolve(h, np.flip(p), mode='full')[n-1:n-1+n]
    G = A - B
    sg = a * h * (1.0 - h)
    grad = dx * sg * (G - float(sg.dot(G)))
    return F, grad
```

def _zinit_list (h0, n, rng):
    L = []
    e = 1e-4: hc = np.clip (h0, e, 1 − e): L += [np.log (hc / (1 − hc))]
    L += [(h0 − 0.5) * 5]
    L += [(h0 − 0.5) * 5 + rng.normal (0, 0.35, n)]
    f = [1, 2, 3]: x = np.arange (n)/n: L += [np.sin (2π f x + rng.uniform (0, 6.283))]
    L += [rng.normal (0, 1, n)]
    return L

Final polish pair_push:

```python
def pair_push (h, stop, deltas, radius=30):
    n = len (h); bestc = c5val (h, n)
    dxf = 2.0 / n
    for d in deltas:
        while time.time () < stop:
            imp = False
            for i in range (n):
                if time.time () >= stop: break
                j0 = max (1, i - (radius - 1)); j1 = min (n - 1, i + radius)
                for j in range (j0, j1):
                    if i == j: continue
                    if h[i] + d <= 1.0 and h[j] - d >= 0.0:
                        h[i] += d; h[j] -= d
                        c = float (np.max (np.correlate (h, 1.0 - h, 'full'))) * dxf
                        if c < bestc - 1e-15: bestc = c; imp = True
                        else: h[i] -= d; h[j] += d
            if not imp: break
    return h, bestc
```

Looking at this problem, I need to find h with max_k ∫h(x)(1-h(x+k)) minimized. My approach differs from the previous program: instead of SLSQP with explicit constraints, I use a fully-interior parameterization h_i = σ(a·z_i + φ) (which automatically satisfies 0<h<1 and Σh=n/2 via a 1-D Newton solve for φ), optimize over z with L-BFGS-B on an analytically-graduated log-sum-exp smoothed max over ALL 2n-1 lags, with aggressive temperature continuation (both a and α), many diverse restarts (parent-logit, parent-perturbed, sines, random), then cross-n variants, then an active-set epigraph SLSQP polish and directed pair-push finishing.

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_EPS = 1e-5

def _c5v(L, n):
    return float(np.max(L)) * (2.0 / n)

def _c5max(h, n):
    return _c5v(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full'), n)

def _proj(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    t = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(300):
        d = t - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + d * r / s, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + d * h / s, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def _sigv(x):
    y = np.empty_like(x)
    p = x >= 0.0
    y[p] = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-x[p]))
    e = np.exp(x[~p])
    y[~p] = e / (1.0 + e)
    return y

def _phi_newton(z, a):
    n = len(z)
    ph = 0.0
    for _ in range(40):
        s = _sigv(a * z + ph)
        g = s.sum() - 0.5 * n
        if abs(g) < 1e-15:
            break
        gp = float((s * (1.0 - s)).sum())
        if gp < 1e-16:
            break
        ph -= g / gp
    return ph

def _h_of_z(z, a):
    return _sigv(a * z + _phi_newton(z, a))

def _zobj(x, a, al):
    n = len(x)
    h = _h_of_z(x, a)
    L = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    m = float(L.max())
    w = np.exp(al * (L - m))
    tw = w.sum()
    p = w / tw
    A = np.convolve(1.0 - h, p, mode='full')[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    B = np.convolve(h, np.flip(p), mode='full')[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    G = A - B
    sh = h * (1.0 - h)
    g = a * sh * (G - float(np.dot(sh, G)))
    return (m + np.log(tw) / al) * (2.0 / n), g

def _lbfgs(z, a, al, n, mit, tstop):
    res = minimize(_zobj, z, args=(a, al), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                   options={'maxiter': mit, 'ftol': 1e-18, 'gtol': 1e-13})
    z2 = res.x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z2)):
        z2 = z
    if time.time() >= tstop:
        return z2
    return z2

def _anneal(z0, n, tstop, n_stage=10):
    st = [(2.0, 20, 500), (3.0, 60, 500), (4.5, 150, 550), (6.5, 400, 600),
          (9.5, 900, 650), (14.0, 2000, 700), (21.0, 4500, 750),
          (32.0, 9000, 800), (50.0, 18000, 850), (80.0, 36000, 900)]
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=float).copy()
    K = min(n_stage, len(st))
    for s in range(K):
        if time.time() >= tstop - 2.0:
            break
        a, al, mit = st[s]
        z = _lbfgs(z, a, al, n, mit, tstop)
    h = _proj(_h_of_z(z, st[K - 1][0]), n)
    return z, h, _c5max(h, n)

def _eqf(x, n):
    return x[:n].sum() - 0.5 * n

def _eqj(x, n):
    v = np.zeros(n + 1)
    v[:n] = 1.0
    return v

def _epif(x, n):
    return x[n]

def _epij(x, n):
    v = np.zeros(n + 1)
    v[n] = 1.0
    return v

def _inef(x, n, k):
    h = x[:n]
    L = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return x[n] - L[int(k) + n - 1]

def _inej(x, n, k):
    h = x[:n]
    Av = np.zeros(n)
    Bv = np.zeros(n)
    if k >= 0:
        Av[k:] = 1.0 - h[:n - k]
        Bv[:n - k] = h[k:]
    else:
        Av[:n + k] = 1.0 - h[-k:]
        Bv[-k:] = h[:n + k]
    v = np.zeros(n + 1)
    v[:n] = Bv - Av
    v[n] = 1.0
    return v

def _slsqp_polish(h, n, tstop, kmax=16, msmax=250):
    h = _proj(h.copy(), n)
    c5 = _c5max(h, n)
    for it in range(3):
        if time.time() >= tstop - 8.0:
            break
        L = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        tk = int(np.argmax(L))
        ks = sorted(int(k) + n - 1 - (n - 1) for k in np.concatenate(
            (np.argsort(-L)[:kmax], [np.argmax(L)])))
        ks = [int(j) - (n - 1) for j in set(int(v) for v in map(lambda j: int(j), [int(i) for i in np.argsort(-L)[:kmax]] + [int(np.argmax(L))]))]
        x0 = np.concatenate([h, [c5]])
        bnds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(0.1, 1.5)]
        cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _eqf, 'jac': _eqj, 'args': (n,)}]
        for kk in ks:
            cons.append({'type': 'ineq', 'fun': _inef, 'jac': _inej, 'args': (n, kk)})
        res = minimize(_epif, x0, args=(n,), jac=_epij, method='SLSQP',
                       bounds=bnds, constraints=cons,
                       options={'maxiter': msmax, 'ftol': 1e-14})
        xx = res.x if getattr(res, 'success', False) else None
        if xx is None:
            continue
        hnew = _proj(xx[:n].copy(), n)
        cn = _c5max(hnew, n)
        if cn < c5 - 1e-13:
            h, c5 = hnew, cn
    return h, c5

def _pair_push(h, n, tstop, deltas, rad=40):
    h = h.copy()
    c5 = _c5max(h, n)
    for d in deltas:
        while time.time() < tstop - 1.0:
            imp = False
            for i in range(n):
                if time.time() >= tstop - 1.0:
                    break
                j0 = max(0, i - rad)
                j1 = min(n, i + rad + 1)
                for j in range(j0, j1):
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    if h[i] + d <= 1.0 and h[j] - d >= 0.0:
                        h[i] += d
                        h[j] -= d
                        c = _c5max(h, n)
                        if c < c5 - 1e-15:
                            c5 = c
                            imp = True
                        else:
                            h[i] -= d
                            h[j] += d
            if not imp:
                break
    return h, c5

def _starts(h0, n, rng):
    st = []
    hc = np.clip(h0, 1e-3, 1.0 - 1e-3)
    st.append(np.log(hc / (1.0 - hc)))
    p = _proj((hc / (1.0 - hc)) - np.log(10.0) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.9, n), n)
    st.append((p - 0.5) * 5.0)
    st.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.2, n))
    t = 1.0 - 2.0 * np.arange(n) / float(n)
    st.append(1.5 * np.sin(6.2831853 * 2.0 * t + 1.3))
    st.append(1.5 * np.sin(6.2831853 * 3.0 * t + 2.1) + 0.3 * np.sin(6.2831853 * 7.0 * t))
    st.append((h0 - 0.5) * 5.0 + 0.12 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    return st

def _try_candidate(hc, n, t_end, rng, n_stage, kmax):
    h0 = _proj(hc.sub(_eps) + _eps, n) if hasattr(hc, 'sub') else _proj(hc, n)
    h0 = _proj(np.clip(hc.copy(), 0.0, 1.0), n)
    bc, bh = _c5max(h0, n), h0.copy()
    zs = _starts(h0, n, rng)
    for z0 in zs:
        t_stop = time.time() + 62.0
        if t_stop > t_end - 20.0:
            t_stop = t_end - 20.0
        if time.time() >= t_stop - 3.0:
            break
        zz, h, c = _anneal(z0, n, t_stop, n_stage)
        if c < bc - 1e-14:
            bc, bh = c, h
        else:
            h, c = _slsqp_polish(h, n, min(t_stop, t_end - 20.0), kmax, 220)
            if c < bc - 1e-14:
                bc, bh = c, h
    if time.time() < t_end - 30.0:
        bh, bc = _slsqp_polish(bh, n, min(time.time() + 40.0, t_end - 15.0), kmax, 320)
    if time.time() < t_end - 12.0:
        dl = [0.05, 0.01, 0.002, 0.0004] if n <= 260 else [0.05, 0.008, 0.0015]
        bh, bc = _pair_push(bh, n, min(time.time() + 26.0, t_end - 4.0), dl,
                            60 if n <= 260 else 36)
    bh = _proj(bh, n)
    bc = _c5max(bh, n)
    return bh, bc

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    t_end = t0 + float(budget_s) - 6.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    try:
        h_par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h_par = np.full(250, 0.5)
    n_p = len(h_par)
    h_par = _proj(h_par, n_p)
    c_p = _c5max(h_par, n_p)
    B_h, B_c, B_n = h_par.copy(), c_p, n_p

    def upd(h, c, n):
        nonlocal B_h, B_c, B_n
        c = _c5max(_proj(h, n), n)
        if c < B_c - 1e-15:
            B_h, B_c, B_n = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0).copy(), c, n

    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_p, endpoint=False)
    ns_list = [n_p]
    for n2 in (320, 400, 256, 200):
        if n2 != n_p and ns_list.count(n2) == 0:
            ns_list.append(n2)
    for n2 in ns_list:
        if time.time() >= t_end - 110.0:
            break
        stg = 10 if n2 <= 330 else 7
        km = 16 if n2 <= 330 else 12
        if n2 == n_p:
            hc = h_par
        else:
            xt = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n2, endpoint=False)
            hc = _proj(np.interp(xt, xs, h_par), n2)
        hh, cc = _try_candidate(hc, n2, t_end, rng, stg, km)
        upd(hh, cc, n2)

    if time.time() < t_end - 40.0:
        xb = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, B_n, endpoint=False)
        hh, cc = _try_candidate(_proj(B_h, B_n), B_n, t_end, rng, 6, 16)
        upd(hh, cc, B_n)

    B_h = _proj(B_h, B_n)
    B_c = _c5max(B_h, B_n)
    return B_h, B_c, B_n
```
